# airs-cli-rs compatibility checklist

This checklist records, behaviour by behaviour, how `airs-cli-rs` compares with the
TypeScript `airs-cli` and which executable tests pin each answer. It is derived from
the test suites (`cargo test --workspace -- --list`), the command implementations in
`src/cli.rs` and `src/commands/`, and the TypeScript reference sources and tests.

[DESIGN.md](DESIGN.md) stays the contract. This file does not specify behaviour; it
cross-references it. When the two disagree, DESIGN wins and this file is wrong.

## 1. How to read this file

**TypeScript sources.** TypeScript paths are relative to `.reference/prisma-airs-cli/`,
the pinned reference build (commit `7a54e46`, tag `v7.3.0`, package `7.3.0`, SDK
`@cdot65/prisma-airs-sdk` 0.35.0; from 8.0, the final reference, docs/TESTING.md §3.4;
until 8.0 commit `5711012`, package `7.2.1-forgejo.0`, SDK 0.34.0). Its `src/` is
byte-identical to this repository's history at that commit. Every `file:line` citation
was refreshed for 7.3.0: only the files 7.3.0 changed moved, and each moved range was
mapped through the diff and re-read. "Observed" means the behaviour was measured by running the
reference build against the loopback fixture (`docs/research/ts-baseline-observed.md`);
every parity test re-observes it on each run.

**Status values.**

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Identical — parity tested | A `tests/parity_*.rs` test runs both executables against the same fixture and asserts the same observable result (exit code, stdout, the compared stderr, requests). |
| Divergent — Dnn | An intentional difference recorded in DESIGN §12 row Dnn. Where a parity test exists it asserts both sides. |
| Rust-only | Behaviour the TypeScript CLI does not have (a flag, an output format, a local check). Pinned by Rust-side tests only. |
| Not ported (Mn) | TypeScript behaviour outside release 1, with the milestone that plans it (`docs/MILESTONES.md`), or "not planned". |

**Test references.** A test is written as a backticked path from the repository root,
then its module path and function name, for example
`tests/parity_scan.rs::divergence::d6_failed_scans_fail_closed` or
`src/pagination.rs::tests::collect_limits`. `tests/docs_consistency.rs` checks every
such reference: the file must exist, each module must be declared in it, and the
function must be a `#[test]` inside that module. It also checks that every DESIGN §12
row appears in the divergence index (§4).

**Tiers.** Unit tests live in `src/` (`cargo test --lib`). Loopback executable tests in
`tests/*.rs` spawn the real binary against the in-process fixture server; the
`tests/parity_*.rs` files also spawn the TypeScript reference. Harness self-tests are in
`crates/airs-test-harness/tests/`. Nothing in these tiers leaves `127.0.0.1`. The live
tier is separate and opt-in (§6.2).

Global flags (accepted before or after the subcommand): `--output <format>`, `--quiet`,
`--debug` (the API log, §3.10), `--tenant <name>` (Rust-only, D33), `-V`/`--version`,
`-h`/`--help`. Every command that
makes requests exits 130 on SIGINT and 143 on SIGTERM (D32). A command group without a
subcommand prints its help to stderr and exits 2 (D1).

## 5. TypeScript integration specs and their Rust coverage

### 5.1 `tests/integration/sdk-runtime-contract.spec.ts`

This spec is an SDK contract test. It stubs global `fetch`, calls `init()` and
`Scanner.asyncScan` directly, and never runs the CLI.

| TS case | What it pins | Rust coverage |
|---|---|---|
| `makes exactly one async POST attempt when per-call retries are disabled` (`:19-32`) | A network failure on `asyncScan` with `numRetries: 0` rejects with `failureKind: 'network'` after one `fetch`, overriding a global `numRetries: 5`. | Async submission is out of scope until M5. The equivalent release-1 rule is stronger: the sync scan POST is never replayed, whatever `airsNumRetries` says: `tests/parity_scan.rs::divergence::d9_scan_post_never_replayed`, `tests/scan.rs::http_errors::status_503_with_num_retries_5_is_still_one_post`, `tests/scan.rs::http_errors::connection_refused`. |
| `exposes confirmed HTTP 429 status and normalized Retry-After metadata` (`:34-54`) | A 429 with `Retry-After: 2` rejects with `statusCode: 429`, `retryAfterMs: 2000` after one call. | One POST and the `HTTP 429` line for the sync scan: `tests/parity_scan.rs::parity_errors::status_429_empty_body_same_error_and_one_post`, `tests/scan.rs::http_errors::status_429_is_not_retried`. `retryAfterMs` is not surfaced by any release-1 command; it matters for bulk scanning (M5). |
| `accepts the SDK 0.13.2 maximum of 20 async request objects` (`:56-80`) | `MAX_NUMBER_OF_BATCH_SCAN_OBJECTS === 20`; a 20-object `asyncScan` makes one call. | Not ported (M5: 20-item async submissions). No release-1 test. |

### 5.2 `tests/integration/dlp-tenant-auth.spec.ts`

This spec spawns the real CLI against a loopback `node:http` server with two tenants
(TSG 100 and 200) registered through `tenant create` and `tenant switch`.

| TS case or harness rule | What it pins | Rust coverage |
|---|---|---|
| `loads JSON credentials and the DLP endpoint for %s` (`:138-149`, four DLP groups) | One OAuth POST with the selected tenant's credentials, then one GET attributed to TSG 100 on `dlpEndpoint`. | `tests/dlp_reads.rs::tenants::every_group_uses_the_selected_tenant_and_its_dlp_endpoint` (all four groups, the selected tenant and `--tenant dev`, `mgmtEndpoint` pointed elsewhere and unused, tenant files unchanged), `crates/airs-runtime-core/src/clients.rs::tests::dlp_clients_admit_the_tenant_endpoint_or_the_default`. |
| `creates and reads a pattern and advanced profile in each tenant without changing credentials` (`:151-234`) | Writes in each tenant after `tenant switch`; requests carry only that tenant's TSG; config bytes unchanged afterwards. | `tests/dlp_writes.rs::tenants::writes_use_the_selected_tenant_only` (a pattern create, a profile create over it and a read in each tenant, the selected one and `--tenant dev`; only that TSG on every request; `mgmtEndpoint` unused; tenant files unchanged); topic writes: M4; `tenant switch` is ported (§3.11). The read-only parts: `tests/parity_tenant.rs::accepted::switching_the_registry_active_switches_requests`, `tests/tenant.rs::isolation::switching_the_registry_active_switches_every_request`, and the unchanged-files check on every run, `crates/airs-test-harness/tests/sandbox_runner.rs::runner_fails_a_run_that_changes_any_sandbox_file`. |
| `ignores environment credentials and config paths; only the selected tenant file authenticates` (`:236-245`) | `PANW_MGMT_*`, `PANW_DLP_ENDPOINT` and `PRISMA_AIRS_CONFIG_PATH` do not change requests. | `tests/dlp_reads.rs::tenants::the_environment_is_ignored` (a DLP read); `tests/parity_tenant.rs::accepted::environment_is_ignored_for_management`, `tests/parity_management.rs::errors::credential_environment_is_ignored`, `tests/tenant.rs::environment::management_requests_use_only_the_file`, `tests/tenant.rs::environment::config_path_variable_does_not_bypass_an_empty_registry`. |
| Harness: `stdout + stderr` never match `/FAKE-SECRET\|mock-token/` (`:32`) | Secret leakage on every invocation. | Checked on every run of both CLIs, in more forms: `crates/airs-test-harness/tests/sandbox_runner.rs::runner_detects_secret_leaks`, `crates/airs-test-harness/tests/sandbox_runner.rs::extra_and_trimmed_secrets_are_checked_in_every_form`. |
| Harness: `result.code ?? 0` (`:31`) | Treats a child killed by the 20 s timeout as exit 0. | Not reproduced: the Rust runner fails a timed-out or signal-killed child: `crates/airs-test-harness/tests/sandbox_runner.rs::runner_kills_and_fails_on_timeout`, `crates/airs-test-harness/tests/sandbox_runner.rs::runner_rejects_unexpected_signal_death`. |
| Harness: environment filtered from `process.env` (`:105-111`) | Drops `PANW_*` and `PRISMA_AIRS_*`. | The Rust runner uses an allowlist instead: `crates/airs-test-harness/tests/sandbox_runner.rs::runner_uses_allowlisted_environment_null_stdin_and_sandbox_cwd`. |

## 6. TypeScript documentation discrepancies

### 6.1 "No tests in this repo hit a live API"

The claim, `docs-site/docs/development/testing.md:48`:

> No tests in this repo hit a live API. For live AIRS coverage, run the manual checklist
> in [Live Smoke Tests](smoke-tests.md) — that's the authoritative way to catch backend
> wire-format drift, especially under SDK 0.8.0's now-on runtime Zod validation.

The same claim appears at `docs-site/docs/development/smoke-tests.md:5` ("Zero tests in
this repo hit a real Prisma AIRS tenant"). Related claims:
`docs-site/docs/development/testing.md:12-13` and `:46` say the SDK clients are never
instantiated and no HTTP request leaves the process, and
`docs-site/docs/development/local-setup.md:32` says unit and integration tests use MSW
mocks.

Counter-evidence in the same repository:

| Evidence | Location |
|---|---|
| Live E2E suites, excluded from `pnpm test` but run by `pnpm test:e2e` | `vitest.config.ts:8-9` (excludes `tests/e2e/**`), `vitest.e2e.config.ts:7` (includes `tests/e2e/**/*.e2e.spec.ts`, no `setupFiles`), `package.json:26-27` (`test:e2e`, `test:e2e:aigateway`) |
| `tests/e2e/aigateway-cli.e2e.spec.ts` | gated by `RUN_AIGATEWAY_E2E=1` and a selected tenant (`:16-17`, `:52`) |
| `tests/e2e/aigateway-dashboard.e2e.spec.ts` | gated by `RUN_AIGATEWAY_DASHBOARD_E2E` (`:10`) |
| `tests/e2e/redteam-dashboard.e2e.spec.ts` | gated by `RUN_REDTEAM_DASHBOARD_E2E` (`:10`) |
| `tests/e2e/runtime-dashboard.e2e.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/runtime-report.e2e.spec.ts` | gated by `RUN_RUNTIME_REPORT_E2E` (`:12`; `:13-15`) |
| Live acceptance scripts with no gate | `scripts/e2e-tenant-profiles.mjs:1-2`, `scripts/e2e-dlp-fallback.mjs`, `scripts/e2e-agentguard.mjs`; `scripts/e2e-full-runtime-migration.mjs:21-27` requires `--execute` and switches the operator's registry selection (`:2`) |
| The repository's own instructions name them | `CLAUDE.md:25` (`pnpm test:e2e  # E2E tests (requires real creds, opt-in)`), `AGENTS.md:24` (`scripts/e2e-agentguard.mjs` for live acceptance), `AGENTS.md:340`, `docs-site/docs/development/local-setup.md:29`, `docs-site/docs/cli/aigateway/resources.md:183-191` |
| `pnpm test` instantiates the real SDK and makes HTTP requests | seven integration specs spawn the CLI against a loopback server, e.g. `tests/integration/dlp-tenant-auth.spec.ts:17-34`, `:103`; `tests/integration/sdk-runtime-contract.spec.ts` calls the real SDK with a stubbed `fetch` |
| No MSW | not a dependency; no test imports it (`docs/research/ts-tests-and-docs.md` §1.1) |

What is true is narrower: `pnpm test` never contacts a non-loopback host. Live coverage
exists, is opt-in per suite, and the scripts have no gate at all.

### 6.2 Resolution adopted by airs-cli-rs

`airs-cli-rs` states its tiers explicitly (DESIGN §10):

1. **Unit tests** (`src/**`, `cargo test --lib`): pure logic, no process, no network.
2. **Loopback executable tests** (`tests/*.rs`, including the `tests/parity_*.rs`
   suites that also run the TypeScript reference): every endpoint of every fixture
   tenant is `http://127.0.0.1:<port>`. The harness refuses a config, a planned
   redirect or a TypeScript environment that could route a request off loopback
   (§3.8, last row). `cargo test --workspace` runs tiers 1 and 2 and never contacts a
   non-loopback host.
3. **Live tier** (`tests/live.rs`): runs only when both `AIRS_RS_LIVE=1` and
   `AIRS_E2E_TENANT=<name>` are set; the tenant is never defaulted to the active one.
   Without both variables it makes no request and reads no tenant file. DESIGN §10 states
   what the tier does and the guarantees it must keep (an isolated copy of one registry
   entry, imported with `airs tenant import` into a private 0700 store under
   `target/live/` that is removed afterwards, unchanged real tenant files, bounded runs,
   sanitized summaries).

The documents in this repository state that condition rather than an absolute: no test
reaches a live API unless both variables are set.

### 6.3 Proposed wording for the TypeScript testing guide

Proposed replacement for `docs-site/docs/development/testing.md:48` (text only; the
TypeScript repository is not edited from here):

> `pnpm test` never contacts a live Prisma AIRS API. Unit specs use the service mocks
> in `tests/helpers/mocks.ts`. Integration specs (`tests/integration/`) spawn the real
> CLI and SDK against a loopback HTTP server with synthetic credentials, or call the
> SDK with a stubbed `fetch`. Live coverage is opt-in: `pnpm test:e2e` runs
> `tests/e2e/*.e2e.spec.ts`, and each suite runs only when its `RUN_*_E2E=1` flag is
> set. It then uses `AIRS_E2E_TENANT=<name>` or the active tenant, and fails if neither
> names a registered tenant; the AI Gateway CLI suite (`aigateway-cli.e2e.spec.ts`)
> skips instead. The `scripts/e2e-*.mjs` acceptance scripts call live APIs whenever they are run. The
> manual [smoke](smoke-tests.md) and [sweep](full-cli-sweep.md) checklists are the
> broadest live check.

The same change should drop "SDK 0.8.0" (the pinned SDK is 0.34.0, `package.json:68`),
limit the "never instantiated" sentences at `testing.md:12-13` and `:46` to unit
specs, add `tests/integration/`, `tests/e2e/` and `tests/fixtures/` to the tree at
`testing.md:18-32` (and rename `airs-cli/` to `airs/`), fix `smoke-tests.md:5` the same
way, and remove the MSW sentence at `local-setup.md:32`.

### 6.4 Other TypeScript documentation discrepancies that affect users

| TS documentation | What the implementation does | airs-cli-rs |
|---|---|---|
| `docs-site/docs/getting-started/exit-codes-and-output.md:11`, `:14-15`: exit 2 is "Usage error — invalid flag value, unparsable input file, missing required flag combination", and "Scripts can rely on `2` meaning \"fix the invocation\"" | commander parse errors (unknown option, missing argument or required option) and bare groups exit 1 (no `exitOverride`); only the CLI's own combination and value checks exit 2 | exit 2 for every usage error (D1) |
| `docs-site/docs/reference/environment-variables.md:52-53`: "the SDK never falls back to them either" | a key-only or token-only tenant passes `undefined` for the other credential (`src/config/client-options.ts:89-97`) and the TS SDK falls back to `PANW_AI_SEC_API_KEY`/`TOKEN` | never (D8) |
| `docs-site/docs/development/smoke-tests.md:19-22`, `full-cli-sweep.md:11`: set `PANW_*` variables or `~/.prisma-airs/config.json`; `local-setup.md:88` passes credentials as `PANW_*`; `local-setup.md:106-108`: "CLI flags > environment variables > config file" | the CLI reads credentials only from tenant files (`src/config/env.ts`) | same rule; doctor lists the ignored names (§3.4) |
| `docs-site/docs/getting-started/quick-start.md:39-41`: missing management credentials and a conflicting environment are `fail` | missing management credentials warn when scanner credentials pass; the Environment check never fails (`src/cli/commands/doctor.ts:233-306`) | same outcomes (parity tested, §3.4) |
| `docs-site/docs/cli/runtime/scan.md:41`, `:64`: `Action:    BLOCK` with colons and a divider | `  Action     BLOCK`, no colon, no divider (`src/cli/commands/runtime.ts:64-82`) | matches the implementation |
| `docs-site/docs/development/full-cli-sweep.md:301`: the `--response` example "tests both prompt and response paths" | `response_detected` is never read (`src/airs/runtime.ts:104`) | response detections are shown (D7) |
| `docs-site/docs/cli/runtime/profiles.md:80`: `Next offset: 2` | stderr `Showing 2 (more available)` | same status line in every format |
| `docs-site/docs/cli/runtime/topics.md:179-180` (get JSON with `cspId`, `tsgId`), `:241` (`Showing 3 of 100 topics`) | `cspId`/`tsgId` dropped; `Showing 3` | same projection (parity tested); honest status (D13) |
| `src/cli/commands/doctor.ts:137` hint: `PRISMA_AIRS_CONFIG_PATH bypasses the registry meanwhile` | the variable is retired and ignored (`src/config/env.ts:19`) | `Restore or remove <registryPath>` (D25) |

## 7. Keeping this file current

- A change that adds or renames a test cited here, or adds a §12 row, updates this file
  in the same change. `tests/docs_consistency.rs` fails otherwise: it checks every
  backticked test reference, that every §12 row has a row in §4, and that every
  `dNN`-named parity test is cited.
- A new divergence gets its §12 row first (AGENTS.md), then a §4 row here naming its
  `dNN_` parity test, or "no executable test — reason".

## 12. Optional runtime MCP client (D350–D361, M7)

The `airs` side of the optional service (RUNTIME_MCP_CONTRACT §7). The loopback suite runs
the real `airs` executable against a fixture gateway and authorization server in front of
the real `airs-mcp` server (`tests/mcp_client.rs`). Each difference also has a `dNNN_`
test in `tests/parity_mcp_client.rs`. It runs a representative invocation on both CLIs: the
Rust exit code and message, and the TypeScript reference's unknown-command or
unknown-option refusal, with no request to the service.

| DESIGN difference | Behavior | Executable coverage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| D350 | `mcp connections set\|list\|remove`; strict `mcp` tenant namespace; rollback snapshot on the first write | `tests/mcp_client.rs::connections_are_strict_tenant_namespace_entries_with_a_rollback_snapshot`, `crates/airs-runtime-core/src/tenant/mcp.rs::tests::connections_round_trip_and_every_rule_is_enforced`, `tests/parity_mcp_client.rs::d350_mcp_connections_are_rust_only` |
| D351 | `mcp login\|logout\|status`; PKCE sign-in; private session cache bound to its connection | `tests/mcp_client.rs::login_then_history_jobs_sync_and_schedules_through_the_gateway`, `crates/airs-mcp-client/src/lib.rs::tests::session_cache_is_private_and_refuses_unsafe_files`, `crates/airs-mcp-client/src/lib.rs::tests::a_session_bound_elsewhere_is_never_reused`, `crates/airs-mcp-client/src/lib.rs::tests::refresh_lock_refuses_symlinks_and_loose_files`, `crates/airs-mcp-client/src/lib.rs::tests::trust_requires_https_or_loopback_and_approved_origins`, `tests/parity_mcp_client.rs::d351_mcp_sign_in_is_rust_only` |
| D352 | `runtime history\|jobs\|schedules\|sync --mcp`: bounded typed calls; noninteractive authentication refusal | `tests/mcp_client.rs::login_then_history_jobs_sync_and_schedules_through_the_gateway`, `crates/airs-mcp-client/src/lib.rs::tests::the_complete_result_bound_counts_duplicated_content`, `tests/parity_mcp_client.rs::d352_service_reads_are_rust_only` |
| D353 | Service commands without `--mcp` are usage errors naming the service; no fallback | `tests/mcp_client.rs::service_commands_need_an_explicit_connection_and_never_fall_back`, `tests/no_local_history.rs::removed_history_commands_and_flags_are_usage_errors_before_any_access`, `tests/parity_mcp_client.rs::d353_service_commands_need_an_explicit_connection` |
| D354 | Durable operation receipts for submissions | `tests/mcp_client.rs::login_then_history_jobs_sync_and_schedules_through_the_gateway`, `crates/airs-mcp-client/src/receipts.rs::tests::receipts_are_private_atomic_bounded_and_listed`, `tests/parity_mcp_client.rs::d354_operation_receipts_are_rust_only`, `tests/mcp_client.rs::a_legacy_receipt_stays_unresolved_but_never_shows_its_job_id`, `tests/mcp_client.rs::status_refuses_an_incomplete_receipt_inventory`, `tests/mcp_client.rs::a_receipt_outlives_an_undelivered_result_and_strict_exits_keep_the_result` |
| D355 | `runtime report --mcp`: archive-only render, verified bundle, `--from-evidence`/budget conflicts | `tests/mcp_client.rs::receipts_reports_and_downloads_through_the_gateway`, `tests/parity_mcp_client.rs::d355_service_reports_are_rust_only` |
| D356 | `runtime history query\|reports\|download`: hash-verified, no-clobber private downloads, screened for the invocation's secrets | `tests/mcp_client.rs::receipts_reports_and_downloads_through_the_gateway`, `tests/parity_mcp_client.rs::d356_report_downloads_are_rust_only`, `tests/mcp_client.rs::a_transferred_file_carrying_a_token_is_never_published`, `src/commands/mcp_ops.rs::tests::a_secret_split_across_chunks_is_found`, `src/commands/mcp_ops.rs::tests::publication_refuses_a_token_registered_after_the_bytes_were_read`, `src/commands/mcp_ops.rs::tests::a_token_registered_mid_transfer_is_still_found` |
| D357 | lifecycle previews, `--execute` with a fresh nonce-bound admin sign-in, `mcp confirm` + `--approval` | `tests/mcp_client.rs::admin_lifecycle_needs_a_fresh_nonce_bound_sign_in_and_keeps_no_session`, `crates/airs-mcp-client/src/previews.rs::tests::preview_records_are_private_named_by_their_id_and_found_across_connections`, `tests/parity_mcp_client.rs::d357_lifecycle_confirmation_is_rust_only` |
| D358 | `history export\|import`: verified export, staged import, admin connection only | `tests/mcp_client.rs::admin_lifecycle_needs_a_fresh_nonce_bound_sign_in_and_keeps_no_session`, `tests/parity_mcp_client.rs::d358_archive_transfer_is_rust_only` |

## 13. Optional runtime MCP service (M0 roadmap)

M0 changes no CLI behavior, so it adds no checklist or divergence rows. The M0
contract (docs/RUNTIME_MCP_CONTRACT.md) freezes what later rows must cover. The
isolated prototype suites (prototypes/runtime-mcp, outside the root workspace and
recorded in docs/RUNTIME_MCP_M0_FEASIBILITY.md) are feasibility evidence, not CLI
coverage, so they are deliberately not cited as checklist tests here.

Fixture inventory that M1–M8 must add, with executable tests, before the matching
behavior is claimed:

| Area | Planned fixtures/suites |
| --- | --- |
| No-MCP independence | CLI package graph without SQLite/server; zero MCP calls and zero state files without explicit selection; direct/offline parity unchanged |
| Usage validation | `--mcp` required for service commands (exit 2 before tenant access); `--from-evidence` × `--mcp` conflict; removed local history flags |
| Identity | Loopback OIDC/JWKS issuer, gateway forwarding fixture, two-token allow/deny matrix (issuer, audience, tenant, client, owner, backend binding, workspace, organisation, lifetime, key rotation, alg/`jku` refusal) |
| Authorization | Tier ∩ grant ∩ allowed-tool intersections; unknown agents; stale/revoked grants; per-request reauthorization; admin disabled without a qualified option; fresh-proof binding/replay/shell-context probes |
| CLI OAuth | PKCE/`state`/`iss`/resource checks, private cache modes, refresh rotation and serialization, lost refresh response, noninteractive refusal, logout scope, interrupted login |
| Jobs | Lost accepted response and relogin idempotency, conflicting digest, disconnect versus cancel, restart checkpoints, overload, control-restored epochs |
| Quotas | Two-send/ten-ID per job and per Chicago day across jobs, restarts and credential changes; 429 cooldowns; clock rollback high-water guard |
| Scheduling | Spring/fall DST bounds, midnight races, duplicate/missed ticks, delayed revisions, fixed +24 h cutoff, evidence-only digests |
| Storage | Crash points C1–C12 (contract §9.2), restore restrictions, low-space pauses, singleton ownership, fsGroup-shaped ancestors refused |
| Artifacts | ≤16 KiB agent pages, hash-verified chunked downloads, no-clobber publication, fences against stale renderers, unauthorized/guessed IDs |
| Receipts | Client-asserted provenance, forged complete identities, both arrival orders, two-second CLI wait, bulk-state upload gaps |
| Transfer/lifecycle | crates/airs-history-store/tests/support/history_transfer_adversarial.rs against the service adapters, all-copy prune previews, migration from read-only staging |
